      "tikz_pgf_source": "\\begin{tikzpicture}[font=\\small,>=Stealth,\n  every path/.style={line width=.6pt}]\n  \\begin{scope}[shift={(0,0)}]\n    \\fill[gray!10] (-1.9,-1.25) rectangle (1.9,1.25);\n    \\draw[->] (-1.7,0) -- (1.7,0) node[right] {$x$};\n    \\draw[->] (0,-1.05) -- (0,1.05) node[above] {$y$};\n    \\fill (0,0) circle (2pt);\n    \\node[align=center] at (0,-1.8)\n      {Body plane $n=v=0$\\\\basis curves meet once};\n  \\end{scope}\n  \\begin{scope}[shift={(6,0)}]\n    \\draw[->,gray] (-2,0) -- (2,0) node[right] {$n$};\n    \\draw[->,gray] (0,-.2) -- (0,1.65) node[above] {$v$};\n    \\draw[->,thick] (0,0) -- (1.3,1.3)\n      node[right,align=left] {$x$-annulus\\\\$n=v=s$};\n    \\draw[->,thick] (0,0) -- (-1.3,1.3)\n      node[left,align=right] {$y$-annulus\\\\$n=-s,\\ v=s$};\n    \\draw[dashed] (-1.6,.75) -- (1.6,.75);\n    \\fill (-.75,.75) circle (2pt);\n    \\fill (.75,.75) circle (2pt);\n    \\node[anchor=west,inner sep=2pt] at (1.65,.75) {$v=s>0$};\n    \\node[align=center] at (0,-1.8)\n      {Normal plane $(n,v)$\\\\separate points at each positive level};\n  \\end{scope}\n\\end{tikzpicture}",
      "witness_description": "Two coordinate plane plots side by side. The left plot is labeled \"Body plane $n = v = 0$\" and shows perpendicular axes labeled $x$ (horizontal) and $y$ (vertical), intersecting at a black dot at the origin, with text below: \"basis curves meet once\". The right plot is labeled \"Normal plane $(n, v)$\" with horizontal axis labeled $n$ and vertical axis labeled $v$. Two rays emerge from the origin $(0,0)$: one into the first quadrant labeled \"$x$-annulus\", \"$n = v = s$\", and one into the second quadrant labeled \"$y$-annulus\", \"$n = -s, v = s$\". A horizontal dashed line at level $v = s > 0$ intersects both rays at marked points. Below is text: \"separate points at each positive level\"."
    },
    {
      "caption": "Attachment tree for one relation and the two ordered compressions. The left panel records attachments; the right panel separates the two outer contracting-disc copies and their inner cap pairs. Signs indicate the opposite induced orientations in each pair. Opposite end cap types may cross in the balls $P_{e}$. The unused $y_{r}$ cap remains in the manifold and will kill the comparison loop in Lemma 8.",
      "figure_id": "geo:fig-stages",
      "pdf_page": 13,
      "source_evidence": [
        {
          "anchor": "geo:fig-stages",
          "exact_quote": "Attachment tree for one relation and the two ordered\ncompressions.  The left panel records attachments; the right panel\nseparates the two outer contracting-disc copies and their inner cap pairs.\nSigns indicate the opposite induced orientati",
          "source_object": "fig:0003"
        }
      ],
      "source_kind": "tikz_pgf",
      "tikz_pgf_source": "\\begin{tikzpicture}[font=\\small,>=Stealth,\n  body/.style={draw,rounded corners,align=center,inner sep=5pt},\n  capnode/.style={draw,rounded corners=8pt,align=center,inner sep=4pt},\n  every path/.style={line width=.5pt}]\n  \\node[body] (base) at (0,0) {$B_r$\\\\$\\partial B_r=R_r$};\n  \\node[capnode] (shallow) at (-1.65,1.8) {shallow cap\\\\$z_a^{c_1}$};\n  \\node[body] (upper) at (1.55,1.8) {$S_r$\\\\$[z_b^{c_2},z_c^{c_3}]$};\n  \\node[capnode] (x) at (.3,3.6) {$x_r$ cap\\\\$z_b^{c_2}$};\n  \\node[capnode] (y) at (2.9,3.6) {$y_r$ cap\\\\$z_c^{c_3}$};\n  \\draw (base) -- node[pos=.33,left] {$\\alpha_r$} (shallow);\n  \\draw (base) -- node[pos=.33,right] {$\\beta_r$} (upper);\n  \\draw (upper) -- node[left] {$x_r$} (x);\n  \\draw (upper) -- node[right] {$y_r$} (y);\n  \\draw[dashed] (-2.65,.8) -- (3.6,.8);\n  \\node[align=center] at (.4,-.8) {Only the base tube is deleted.};\n\n  \\node[align=center] at (7,4.15)\n    {$S_r\\longrightarrow D_r$, then $T_r\\longrightarrow g_r$\\\\\n     Four retained $x_r$ sheets in $g_r$};\n  \\draw[rounded corners] (4.9,1.3) rectangle (6.7,3.5);\n  \\draw[rounded corners] (7.3,1.3) rectangle (9.1,3.5);\n  \\node at (5.8,3.15) {$D_r^{+}$};\n  \\node at (8.2,3.15) {$D_r^{-}$};\n  \\draw (5.8,2.5) ellipse (.55 and .22);\n  \\draw (5.8,1.8) ellipse (.55 and .22);\n  \\draw (8.2,2.5) ellipse (.55 and .22);\n  \\draw (8.2,1.8) ellipse (.55 and .22);\n  \\node at (5.8,2.5) {$+$};\n  \\node at (5.8,1.8) {$-$};\n  \\node at (8.2,2.5) {$-$};\n  \\node at (8.2,1.8) {$+$};\n  \\node[align=center] at (7,.6)\n    {Pair sheets within each $D_r$ copy.\\\\\n     Opposite signs have equal Wall labels.};\n  \\node[align=center] at (7,-.65)\n    {The unused $y_r$ cap remains in $M$\\\\\n     and kills the label-comparison loop.};\n\\end{tikzpicture}",
      "witness_description": "Two panels showing attachment and compression structures. The left panel displays a hierarchical attachment tree: at the bottom is a box labeled \"$B_r$\" with \"$\\partial B_r = R_r$\" along a dashed horizontal line marking points $\\alpha_r$ and $\\beta_r$. An arrow leads from $\\alpha_r$ to a rounded box \"shallow cap\" labeled \"$z_a^{c_1}$\". An arrow leads from $\\beta_r$ to an oval \"$S_r$\" labeled \"$[z_b^{c_2}, z_c^{c_3}]$\", which connects via arrows labeled $x_r$ and $y_r$ to an \"$x_r$ cap\" labeled \"$z_b^{c_2}$\" and a \"$y_r$ cap\" labeled \"$z_c^{c_3}$\". Below the panel is text: \"Only the base tube is deleted.\" The right panel is titled \"$S_r \\longrightarrow D_r$, then $T_r \\longrightarrow g_r$\" and \"Four retained $x_r$ sheets in $g_r$\". It displays two vertical stadium shapes labeled \"$D_r^+$\" and \"$D_r^-$\". Inside $D_r^+$ are two circles with signs $+$ (top) and $-$ (bottom). Inside $D_r^-$ are two circles with signs $-$ (top) and $+$ (bottom). To the right is text: \"Pair sheets within each $D_r$ copy. Opposite signs have equal Wall labels.\" and \"The unused $y_r$ cap remains in $M$ and kills the label-comparison loop.\""
    }
  ],
